國際經濟金融週報內容摘要 
美 
國 
PMI 大幅走升、新屋銷售回穩，消費信心薄弱，美中關係暫現緩和訊號 
經濟 
⚫ S&P Global 公布，9 月美國採購經理人綜合指數(PMI Composite Output 
Index)初值由上月的 56.0 升至 58.4，創逾 5 年新高；服務業及製造業
PMI 分別升至 58.7 及 57.0。在內需強勁帶動下，新訂單與就業同步成
長，惟供應鏈瓶頸及能源成本攀升推升通膨壓力。 
⚫ 8 月美國新屋銷售年化率為 68.4 萬戶，月增 6.4%、年減 2.0%，顯示銷
售較前月回升但需求尚未完全恢復。中西部與南部為主要支撐，惟房價
年減、庫存仍維持 48 萬戶以上，供給相對充裕，後續銷售能否延續仍
待觀察。 
⚫ 9 月美國消費者信心指數降至 48.1，月減 7.0%、年減 12.7%，創近四個
月低點。當前狀況與預期指數同步下滑，反映高物價、燃料價格與貿易
不確定性持續壓抑信心；同時通膨預期升溫，後續消費動能仍待觀察。 
重要 
事件 
⚫ 中國國家主席習近平於 9 月 23 日至 25 日訪美並與川普舉行峰會 ， 雙方
同意延長貿易休兵至 2027 年 1 月 10 日，推動部分商品降稅、建立 AI
對話機制及持續磋商稀土供應議題，並延續高層溝通。台灣議題未列入
成果清單，關稅、科技競爭及供應鏈等核心分歧仍未化解，後續談判發
展仍待觀察。 
短評 
⚫ 美國 9 月 PMI 強勁擴張，顯示企業活動與就業動能仍具韌性，但投入
成本與售價上升也反映通膨壓力升溫。相較之下，房市需求尚未全面恢
復，消費信心則明顯轉弱，顯示內需前景仍有隱憂；美中延長貿易休兵
雖有助穩定市場，但核心分歧未解，後續不確定性仍高。 
歐 
元 
區 
9 月景氣增強，進一步推高升息預期 
經濟 ⚫ 歐元區 9 月綜合採購經理人指數(PMI)增 1.1 點至 53.1，服務業 PMI 增1.4 點至 53，製造業 PMI 則持平於 52.7。 
短評 
⚫ 儘管 9 月經濟表現優於預期，但通膨壓力也同步升溫，再加上先前歐洲
央行的鷹派態度，使得 10 月升息一碼的可能性些微增加，亦強化未來
升息預期。

美中貿易延長休戰，惟後續協商成效仍待觀察 
經濟 ⚫ 1-8 月工業部門營業收入、利潤年增率分別為 6.6%、17.6%。 
重要 
事件 
⚫ 習近平赴華府與美國總統川普舉行峰會，雙方同意貿易休戰期延長至
2027 年 1 月 10 日，並成立雙邊貿易理事會，但在科技管制、稀土供應
及關稅等核心議題上，仍未取得突破。 
⚫ 美中雙方就「300 億美元對 300 億美元」對等降稅架構達成共識，包
含中國製造的小型家電、玩具、節日裝飾品及兒童汽車安全座椅等，以
及美國生產的農產品、海鮮、原木、化妝品及醫療器材等。超過90%的
清單內產品將免除雙方此前相互加徵的關稅，並適用最惠國關稅待遇。 
短評 
⚫ 美中雙方貿易休戰期只延長兩個月，反映美國仍保留談判槓桿，持續給
予中國稀土供應履約的壓力。 
⚫ 美中每年貿易總額逾 4,000 億美元，且降稅產品並非半導體、先進製程
設備、電動車、軍民兩用技術等關鍵領域，本次降稅實質效益有限。 
東 
(南) 
亞 
亞銀上修亞洲及太平洋經濟體 2026 年 GDP 預估至 4.7% 
經濟 
⚫ 亞銀預估 2026 年印度 GDP 為 7.0%，通膨率為 5.0%。 
⚫ 亞銀預估 2026 年東協國家 GDP 為 4.7%，其中越南 GDP 達 7.8%表現
最佳。 
重要 
事件 ⚫ 印尼央行 9 月會議第三度維持基準利率於 5.75%不變。 
短評 ⚫ AI 驅動的科技投資仍是亞洲經濟體最重要的成長動能來源，而能源、糧食及氣候風險將成為影響各國通膨與信用風險的重要變數。

115.9.29 
一、 美國—PMI 大幅走升、新屋銷售回穩，消費信心薄弱，美中關係暫現緩和
訊號 
標普全球 (S&P Global) 公布， 2026 年 9 月美國採購經理人綜合指數 (PMI 
Composite Output Index)初值由上月的 56.0 升至 58.4， 連續第四個月攀升 ， 創2021
年 7 月以來新高；其中服務業採購經理人指數(Services PMI)由 56.5 升至 58.7，創
59 個月高點，製造業採購經理人指數(Manufacturing PMI) 由 53.9 升至 57.0，創
2022 年 5 月以來最高水準，顯示美國企業活動持續強勁擴張。受國內市場需求穩
健帶動，製造業與服務業新訂單同步加速成長，推升整體產出增速升至逾 5 年來
最快水準，企業亦積極擴增人力，就業成長速度創 2022 年 6 月以來新高。另一
方面，積壓訂單增加及供應鏈延遲加劇，反映產能利用率持續攀升；加以近期油
價上漲推升燃料及運輸成本，使企業投入成本增幅升至 2022 年 10 月以來最高，
帶動售價漲幅回升，顯示通膨壓力有所升溫。儘管如此，企業對未來一年營運前
景仍維持正向看法，反映市場對美國經濟韌性及需求持續擴張仍具信心。 
2026 年 8 月美國新屋銷售經季節性調整年化率為 68.4 萬戶 ， 較上月增加6.4%，
但較去年同期下降 2.0%，顯示新屋銷售較前月回升，惟仍低於去年同期水準。地
區別表現分歧，其中中西部及南部分別較上月增加 84.9%及 6.9%，東北部及西部
則分別減少 36.1%及 15.2%。價格方面，8 月新屋銷售中位價為 39.37 萬美元，較
上月小升 0.4%，但較去年同期下降 5.8%。供給方面，8 月底經季節性調整後待售
新屋數量為 48.3 萬戶，與上月持平，較去年同期減少 2.0%；按目前銷售速度估
算，供給月數為 8.5 個月，低於上月的 9.0 個月，與去年同期大致持平。整體而
言，8 月美國新屋銷售較前月回升，主要受中西部與南部銷售增加帶動，惟仍低
於去年同期，顯示需求動能尚未完全恢復。價格方面，中位價較去年同期下滑，
反映市場價格仍有調整壓力；同時待售新屋維持在 48 萬戶以上，供給月數仍達
8.5 個月，顯示整體供給相對充裕，後續仍須觀察銷售回升是否具持續性。 
密西根大學公布，2026 年 9 月美國消費者信心指數終值降至 48.1，低於上月
的 51.7， 月減7.0%、 年減12.7%， 為近四個月低點 ， 並較2026 年 1 月下降約 15%，
顯示消費者信心持續轉弱。分項來看，當前經濟狀況指數由51.9 降至 50.9，月減

1.9%、 年減15.7%； 消費者預期指數則由51.5 降至 46.3， 月減10.1%、 年減10.4%，
反映民眾對未來景氣與個人財務前景的看法明顯惡化。調查指出，消費者對高物
價的擔憂持續升高，當前與未來一年個人財務看法均較上月轉弱約一成；短期企
業展望也因燃料價格偏高與貿易爭端再度升溫而明顯下滑。政治立場方面，各陣
營受訪者普遍認為年初以來經濟前景轉弱，其中共和黨人信心較 1 月下降 20%，
民主黨人下降 13%。 通膨預期方面 ， 未來一年通膨預期由8 月的 4.0%升至 4.6%，
為今年 6 月以來最高，且高於今年 2 月的 3.4%(美伊爆發衝突之前)及 2024 年各
月水準；長期通膨預期亦由 3.3%升至 3.4%，結束連續三個月持平，並高於 2024
年 2.8%至 3.2%的區間。整體而言，9 月美國消費信心再度降溫，顯示高物價、燃
料價格與貿易不確定性持續壓抑民眾信心，後續仍須觀察通膨預期升溫是否進一
步影響消費動能。 
中國國家主席習近平於 2026 年 9 月 23 日至 25 日訪問美國，並於 9 月 24 日
在華府白宮與美國總統川普舉行峰會。會後，美中雙方未發布聯合聲明，而是分
別公布成果清單，顯示雙方在穩定雙邊關係及維持高層溝通方面取得部分共識，
包括同意建構「基於尊重、公平、對等的建設性戰略穩定關係」、推動 300 億美
元非敏感商品對等降稅安排、建立人工智慧 (AI)或「超級智慧」對話與風險溝通
機制，以及持續就稀土與關鍵礦產供應鏈議題進行磋商。此外，美方並表示中國
將於 2027 年及 2028 年分別進口至少 1,000 萬公噸美國煤炭。值得注意的是，台
灣議題並未出現在雙方公布的成果文件中；另一方面，中方則額外強調兩軍危機
溝通機制及合作搜尋失蹤美軍遺骸等事項。整體而言，本次川習會有助延續美中
對話與降低短期緊張情勢，惟雙方在關稅、科技競爭、供應鏈安全及其他戰略議
題上的結構性分歧仍未完全化解，後續談判進展及雙邊互動方向仍有待持續觀察。 
二、歐元區—9 月景氣增強，進一步推高升息預期 
 歐元區 9 月綜合採購經理人指數(PMI)增 1.1 點至 53.1，且為連續四個月上
升。其中服務業PMI 增 1.4 點至 53，創近 10 個月高點，主要反映 AI 與國防投資
活動增加，帶動商業服務需求，抵銷能源價格上漲對消費購買力的負面影響。製
造業 PMI 則持平於 52.7，不過，德國萊茵河水位下降，沿岸較為發達的化工、製
藥業物流成本增加，因而暫時壓抑製造業上行空間。值得注意的是，由於國際原
油價格回升，綜合投入、產出物價指數皆上升至近 4 個月最高。故儘管 9 月經濟
表現優於預期，但通膨壓力也同步升溫，再加上先前歐洲央行的鷹派態度，使得

三、日本—9 月綜合 PMI 維持擴張，製造業表現仍具韌性 
 日本 9 月綜合 PMI 由 53.5 降至 52.5，製造業與服務業同步回落，經濟成長動
能較前兩個月的高檔有所降溫。製造業 PMI 下跌 0.8 點至 54.1，雖然產出、新訂
單及出口訂單增速放緩，但外部需求仍相當強勁，尤其 AI 相關需求可能持續帶
動出口表現，加上企業開始重建庫存、供應鏈瓶頸尚未完全解除，預料補庫存需
求將在未來數月繼續支撐生產成長。服務業 PMI 下降 0.9 點至 51.6，受到工資、
運輸及能源成本上升影響 ， 需求動能略有降溫 ， 但積壓訂單與出口業務有所改善 。
另，投入成本與售價漲幅均較前期放緩，顯示通膨壓力正逐步緩解，但整體物價
水準仍維持高檔。此外，企業對未來景氣的信心持續改善，未來活動指數升至七
個月高點，反映企業普遍預期經濟擴張趨勢仍可望延續。 
四、中國—美中貿易延長休戰，惟後續協商成效仍待觀察 
1-8 月工業部門營業收入、利潤年增率分別為 6.6%、17.6%，其中營業收入年
增率較上期(1-7 月)略增 0.1 個百分點，但利潤年增率下降 1.9 個百分點。受惠於
金屬、塑化原物料上漲，加上 AI 需求擴張，以及政府推動高科技產業發展等因
素，營收成長貢獻度較高者包含有色金屬加工(營收、利潤年增 31.1%、71.8%)、
計算機、通信和其他電子設備製造業(營收、利潤年增20.1%、109.9%)、化學原料
及製品(營收、利潤年增 9.1%、51%)。此外，電氣機械及器材業、汽車業營收年
增率雖分別為 11.5%、2.9%，但獲利分別年減 5.2%、16%。非金屬礦物製品業營
收、獲利下滑 9.5、46.7%，黑色金屬加工業營收、獲利分別年減 1.1%、62.4%，
反映產業持續進行減產所致。整體而言，部分產業受惠於短期原物料價格波動，
以及受到政策推動的高科技產業表現較佳，惟部分產業因陷入產能調整週期，或
政府減少支持等因素而表現較弱，產業分化明顯。 
展望未來，儘管美中雙方互相降低等值 300 億美元產品關稅，惟考量雙方每
年貿易總額逾 4,000 億美元，且降稅產品並非半導體、先進製程設備、電動車、
軍民兩用技術等關鍵領域，因此實質效益有限。另外，雖然美中雙方同意延長貿
易休戰，但休戰期只延長兩個月，反映美國仍保留談判槓桿，持續給予中國稀土
供應履約的壓力。後續將關注美中能否在休戰期間就開放市場、科技競爭、供應

鏈安全等議題取得進一步共識，若談判不如預期，美國仍可能重新祭出新的反制

制恐進一步升級，對中國出口形成額外壓力。 
五、印度及東南亞—亞銀上修亞洲及太平洋經濟體 2026 年 GDP 預估至 4.7% 
亞 洲 開 發 銀 行 (ADB) 最 新 經 濟 展 望 (Asian Development Outlook, ADO 
September 2026 )上修 2026 年亞洲及太平洋地區經濟體 2026 年經濟成長率(GDP)
至 4.7%(7 月預估 4.5%)、2027 年為 4.5%。ADB 認為，亞洲開發中經濟體雖面臨
中東衝突延長、俄烏戰爭再升溫、能源價格高檔震盪及強烈聖嬰現象等逆風，但
在政府刺激政策、民間投資及 AI 帶動科技出口支撐下，區域經濟仍展現韌性。
通膨方面，2026 年通膨率下修至 3.9%，惟能源與糧食價格壓力仍高，2027 年通
膨預測上修至 3.3%。 
區域經濟表現方面 ， 南亞成長最強勁 ，2026 年 GDP 成長率預估上修至 6.4%，
主要受印度內需與投資帶動；東亞則受中國內需疲弱影響，成長維持平穩；東協
國家(ASEAN)上調至 4.7%， 其中越南 、 馬來西亞及泰國受惠於科技與製造業投資
而獲上修。中亞及西亞、太平洋地區則面臨能源及食品價格上漲壓力，成長預測
略為下修至 3.7%、3.0%。(詳下表) 
ADB 認為未來最大風險包括地緣政治衝突升級、聖嬰 現象造成糧食供應衝
擊，以及 AI 相關資產可能出現價格重估。整體而言，AI 驅動的科技投資仍是亞
洲經濟體最重要的成長動能來源，而能源、糧食及氣候風險將成為影響各國通膨
與信用風險的重要變數。

五、台灣—AI 浪潮推升接單動能，外銷訂單再創新高 
8 月外銷訂單達 1,029.6 億美元，首度突破千億美元大關，其中受惠於 ASIC
晶片需求帶動，資通產品接單翻倍、電子產品接單亦大增 83.9%，惟光學器材因
面板需求維持低檔，年減 5%。非科技產品方面，基本金屬年增 26.8%，係因銅箔
及銅箔基板受惠 AI 相關供應鏈需求及銅價續漲，加以鍍鋅鋼需求回溫；機械年
增 28.3%，係因半導體業者增加資本支出，帶動半導體設備、自動化設備需求延
續；化學品年增 15.4%，主因藥品接單成長，加上產業用化學品需求持續增加；
塑橡膠製品年增 6.7%， 主係塑膠原料受原料上漲而推升產品售價 ， 加上部分塑膠
製品訂單回溫。展望未來，經濟部統計處預估 2026 年外銷訂單有望突破 1 兆美
元，在 AI 相關需求持續擴張帶動下，科技產品表現可望維持強勁，而傳統產業
中下游產品亦可望受惠於相關供應鏈需求，惟上游原物料價格競爭激烈，後續仍
須關注第四季消費旺季是否有相關備貨需求。

Greater China Technology Hardware

Industry View In-Line
In this report, we focus on preliminary JMTBA orders, which we
believe could be a catalyst for AirTAC's and Hiwin's share prices.
According to the Japan Machine Tool Builders' Association (JMTBA), headline
orders grew 13% MoM and 60% YoY in September 2026 (vs. +2% MoM/+65%
YoY in August 2026), to ¥223bn.

• Domestic orders were +24% MoM/+46% YoY (vs. -3% MoM/+62% YoY in
August), at ¥64bn.
• Overseas orders were +9% MoM/+67% YoY (vs. +5% MoM/+66% YoY in
August), at ¥159bn.
Our view:
• We think the sequential growth in overseas orders was consistent with
seasonality, while the strength on a YoY basis continued to point to an
upcycle scenario.
• Within overseas orders, we think China and Europe may have outperformed,
as manufacturing PMIs held up better (50.1/52.9 for China/Europe in

September vs. 49.8/52.7 in August). N.A is likely to be softer compared to
other regions, as its manufacturing PMI edged down slightly to 54.5 in
September (vs. 54.6 in August).
• We remain OW on both AirTAC (1590.TW) and Hiwin (2049.TW) as we see
company-specific drivers on top of the industry recovery trend:
° Airtac should continue to benefit from its own market share gains, and
valuation appears undemanding at 18x 2027e P/E (vs. 25x average since

2020).
° Hiwin should  see margin expansion in the coming quarters, driven by
higher utilization and price hikes, which we believe could be share price
catalysts. Current valuation of 29x 2027 P/E implies upside vs. peak
cycle valuation at 35-40x P/E. Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of
interest that could affect the objectivity of Morgan Stanley

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
AirTAC International (1590.TW)
Base case, 29x 2027e P/E. We believe this methodology better captures earnings growth
momentum for industrial automation component plays (market sentiment moves along
with business cycles). As the industry enters an up-cycle, we think applying a near-peak cycle
valuation of 29x P/E is reasonable given the stronger business outlook.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger macroeconomic climate in China

n Stronger-than-expected demand from industrial automation
Risks to Downside
n Sharper economic downturn in China
n Miniature linear guideway business development taking more time
n Larger-than-expected investment in new products but weaker-than-expected demand
Hiwin Technologies Corp. (2049.TW)
Base case, P/E. We think this valuation methodology better captures Hiwin's earnings growth
momentum, as its valuation tends to move with the business cycle. We apply a target multiple

of 38x to our 2027 EPS estimate. We view this multiple as justified by the 47% operating profit
CAGR we project for 2025-28.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger industrial automation demand pickup.
n Market share gains.
n Earlier-than-expected revenue contribution from humanoids.
Risks to Downside
n Weaker industrial automation demand.
n More pricing pressure.
n Market share loss.
n More broad-based impact on the global economy from geopolitical risks.

New capacity announcements do not overturn our bullish view.
AI demand and increasing substrate complexity should keep the
market undersupplied from 2027, supporting further pricing and
margin expansion. We raise earnings estimates and price targets
for Unimicron and NYPCB and reiterate OW on both.
More capacity, but limited near-term relief: Suppliers are stepping up investment,

but several newly announced projects are not scheduled to ramp until late 2028-30
or beyond. Our updated supply-demand model continues to indicate undersupply
from 2027 through 2030, with tighter supply in CY27-28, which should only be
more favorable to pricing. Therefore, capacity additions announced in the past few
months do not, in our view, diminish the magnitude of pricing gains over the next
two years.
CY27 pricing should accelerate, amid tighter supply-demand in CY27-28, but

visibility on the magnitude remains limited: We see ABF substrate prices rising
25%+ in CY27, below buy-side expectations of ~50%. Our supply/demand outlook
points to undersupply next year, with larger substrates, higher layer counts, and
greater manufacturing complexity further constraining usable output. Raw material
cost pass-through was a key driver of CY26 price increases, we expect supply
tightness to play a greater role in CY27. This should allow pricing to outpace cost

inflation, driving faster margin expansion. We remain constructive on pricing, but the
magnitude and timing of increases depends on customer-level negotiations.
With pricing a segment-wide tailwind, we prefer Unimicron for its stronger CY27
capacity growth: As supply tightens, the ability to deliver incremental volume
should become a key differentiator. We favor suppliers bringing capacity online in
CY27 over those whose expansion plans contribute in later years. Unimicron’s

Guangfu II ramp should add 10-15% to capacity in CY27, compared with 5-10%
growth at NYPCB through debottlenecking, giving Unimicron greater scope to
benefit from both pricing and volume growth. Unimicron is also our preferred play
heading into earnings, as more demanding expectations for NYPCB leave less room
for an upside surprise. But we remain OW on NYPCB given its networking and AMD
CPU exposure, alongside its proactive pricing strategy.
We reiterate Overweight on Unimicron and NYPCB: Reflecting stronger earnings

trajectories, we raise our EPS estimates for both companies. Based on upside to our
revised price targets, our order of preference is Unimicron (NT$1,775 PT; 24x CY28e
P/E; 41% upside), followed by NYPCB (NT$2,000 PT; 22x CY28e P/E; 39% upside).
Potential risks: 1) Weaker demand for PCs, general servers, and AI servers; 2)
meaningful capacity expansion plans; 3) T-glass shortage; 4) CoWoP displacing ABF
substrates.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.

### Page 2

M
Idea
2
Supply/Demand Update: Undersupply Deepens in CY27–28
Our updated model points to a wider supply shortfall in CY27–28, despite additional
capacity: Incorporating the latest expansion plans and semiconductor demand forecasts,
we now expect ABF substrate demand to exceed available supply by 9.3% in CY27 and
13.7% in CY28, versus 6.2% and 9.4% previously. This represents a widening of 3.1ppt and
4.3ppt, respectively, relative to our prior estimates. We maintain our view that CY26 is a

broadly balanced market, followed by increasingly tight supply over the next two years.
The main demand upgrade comes from other ASICs, including Chinese AI chips: We
raise our shipment assumptions for this category to 6.4mn units in CY27 and 8.7mn in
CY28, from 4.6mn and 6.2mn previously. Our Nvidia GPU and server CPU forecasts remain
largely unchanged, while we lower AMD GPU estimates while also keeping CY27 Trainium
and TPU shipments largely unchanged. The tighter substrate outlook therefore reflects

stronger demand across a broader set of ASIC suppliers, rather than higher shipment
assumptions for every major AI platform. We also raise our substrate content assumptions
for select chips based on our latest supply chain checks, lifting our substrate demand
estimates across some segments, including server CPUs and networking.
Higher supply assumptions provide limited relief in CY27-28: We now include additional
capacity announcements that have been announced recently, including two additional

Kinsus factories, scheduled to come online in CY28 and CY29, beyond the CY27 expansion
already in our model. Several larger projects, however, contribute later. NYPCB’s new site
starts ramping in 2H29, while SEMCO’s Sejong expansion begins mass production in
September 2028 and its project in Vietnam is scheduled to begin operations by June
2030. These additions increase medium-term supply but do not prevent the modeled
shortfall from worsening through CY28.

Increasing substrate complexity adds to the pressure on usable output: Larger body
sizes, higher layer counts, and more demanding manufacturing processes constrain the
output available to meet demand, even as suppliers expand installed capacity. With the
supply gap widening in CY27-28, we expect tightness to become a more important pricing
driver than raw material cost pass-through. This supports our forecast for 25%+ price
increases in CY27 and further increases in CY28, alongside continued margin expansion.

Supply-side developments
Unimicron (3037.TW, OW, covered by Howard Kao)
On September 30, Unimicron announced that it has acquired a Hukou site from
Bridgestone Taiwan for ~NT$10.089bn (~US$318mn). Based on our checks, this is for
capacity expansion plans in 2030 or beyond.
For CY26, Unimicron has announced capex of NT$53.7B.

Over the next few years, Unimicron already has several new production facilities coming
online, including:
•  Guangfu II: ramp in 2027
• Yangmei II: ramp in 2028
• Yangmei III: ramp in 2029
• Yangmei IV: ramp in 2029+
• Bridgestone: ramp in 2030+

### Page 4

M
Idea
4
NYPCB (8046.TW, OW, covered by Howard Kao)
On August 6, NYPCB announced that its Board of Directors had approved a capex budget
of NT$46.8bn (~US$1,474mn) to invest in building a new ABF substrate production site.
Our checks indicate that this production site is located in the southern part of Taiwan, and
will start to ramp up production in 2H29. Given the size of this investment, we estimate
that it will approximately double its capacity exiting CY30, relative to its CY28 capacity.

Kinsus (3189.TW, Not Covered)
In our previous model, we had assumed only one factory (k6b) coming online in CY27,
increasing capacity ~30%. However, recent checks suggest that Kinsus intends to bring
two more factories online, one in CY28 (k6c) and one in CY29 (k6d). This implies that
capacity would increase another ~25% in CY28 and ~20% in CY29, bringing Kinsus' total
ABF substrate capacity to almost double exiting CY29, relative to its CY26 capacity.

Ibiden (4062.T, covered by Shoji Sato)
Back in February, Ibiden first announced that it will invest capex of ¥500bn in the
electronics field planned through F3/29, for ABF substrate expansion. Sato-san's latest
view is that Ibiden will need a new factory building to install the equipment and facilities
that will be purchased with the ¥500bn. As a result, Sato-san expects Ibiden during its 2Q
results briefing (October 30) to announce plans to make a large capital investment,

including for a factory building with a long lead time, and raise its medium-term earnings
forecast (report here).
SEMCO (009150.KS, covered by Shawn Kim)
On September 29, SEMCO announced investment of KRW4.27 trillion (~US$3,178mn) in
Sejong and KRW2.51 trillion (~US$1,488mn) in Vietnam, for AI server semiconductor
substrate capacity expansion.
• Sejong: Investment period from September 2026 to May 2028, with mass
production starting in September 2028. Largest single-product investment to date,

serving as key hub for mass production of high-value-added, highly demanding
products, proactively responding to the increasing demand for AI server substrates.
• Vietnam: The Vietnamese subsidiary aims to complete construction and begin
operations by June 2030. It will be operated as a strategic hub to respond to global
volume demand and enhance production efficiency through a stable supply
system.
With this investment, Samsung Electro-Mechanics plans to actively respond to the

growing demand for high-value-added semiconductor substrates in high-growth markets
such as AI servers, HPC, and automotive electronics. In particular, this investment is being
pursued based on medium- to long-term cooperation with global big tech and
semiconductor customers. The investment includes customer funding support and
medium- to long-term volume guarantees; thus, the company expects to strengthen the
competitiveness of its FCBGA business based on a stable demand foundation.

LG Innotek (011070.KS, covered by Ryan Kim)

### Page 5

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 5
According to news reports (link), LG Innotek signed a memorandum of understanding
(MOU) with Haiphong, Vietnam, for an investment to expand its semiconductor substrate
plant. Under the agreement, LG Innotek will push to build a semiconductor substrate
plant in Haiphong.
•  Construction will begin in July 2026 and is scheduled to be completed in May
2027.
• The site spans approximately 330,000 square meters (98,000 pyeong), equivalent

to 45 soccer fields.
• According to a news report, LG Innotek will invest approximately $1 billion, or
about 1.53 trillion won, for its Phase 1 expansion plan, which mainly includes RF-SiP
(radio frequency system-in-package) and FC-CSP (flip chip-chip scale package).
• ABF substrates will be part of the Phase 2 expansion plan, but no details or
investment size has been disclosed, yet.
According to a press release from LG Innotek on September 30, LG Innotek started mass

production of ABF substrates for PC chipsets targeting big tech clients last year. It
initiated high-volume mass production of FC-BGA for PC CPUs in the third quarter of this
year. Simultaneously, the company is rapidly boosting revenue by securing additional
major clients. Starting next year, it plans to sequentially enter the AI accelerator and AI
server markets.
• Revenue from LG Innotek's package solution business has maintained an upward
trajectory for three consecutive years. With the package solution business

demonstrating tangible results alongside its optical solution business, the
company has strengthened its capacity to generate stable earnings.
• LG Innotek plans to rapidly expand its market share in the emerging semiconductor
substrate market, aiming to build its package solution business, including
semiconductor substrates, into a business generating US$743 million in operating
profit by 2031.
• In the future, LG Innotek plans to develop its future businesses, including

semiconductor substrates and physical AI solutions, to US$5.94 billion by 2030.
The company aims to have such future new businesses account for 25% of total
revenue, thereby completing a well-balanced business portfolio.
The expanded plant will produce major semiconductor substrates including radio
frequency system-in-package (RF-SiP), flip chip chip scale package (FC-CSP), and flip chip
ball grid array (FC-BGA). "The Gumi site will serve as a 'mother factory' responsible for

developing new semiconductor substrate technologies and producing new models and
high-value-added products, while the expanded plant in Vietnam will be operated as a
production base for general-purpose semiconductor substrates," an LG Innotek official
explained.
AT&S (ATSV.VI, Not Covered)
On June 15, AT&S announced that it had signed key terms with AMD and Marvell to add
AI/HPC IC substrate capacity in Kulim, Malaysia. The company said the €1.5-2bn

investment (approximately US$1.68bn to US$2.24bn) is fully supported and financed by
long-term customer commitments, pending final execution.

Shinko (private)
On September 30, Shinko announced that it has entered into a purchase agreement with
Japan Display Inc. (JDI) to acquire its Mobara fab. In addition, it has entered into a sale
agreement with FICT Ltd. for the transfer of a portion of the fab to be acquired.
• Shinko will position the fab as a new core plant for advanced semiconductor
packages, and strengthen its production systems for flip-chip type packages
through the determination and implementation of capital investment plans for the

fab in conjunction with capacity expansion at its plants in the Nagano area.
Through these initiatives, Shinko aims to respond accurately to strong market
demand while pursuing sustainable business growth and enhancing corporate
value.
• The JDI Mobara fab has a total land area of 373,483 square meters, with Shinko
using the eastern area of the Fab (140,738 square meters, or ~38%) and FICT
planning to use the western area (232,745 square meters, or ~62%).

Toppan (7911.T, Not Covered)
According to news, Toppan broke ground in March 2024 on a new factory on Pesawat
Drive near Singapore's Jurong Lake District, built through its local unit Advanced Substrate
Technologies (AST), a JV with Broadcom. It makes FC-BGA substrates, which is Toppan's
term for them. It is Toppan's first FC-BGA plant outside Japan. The plant opening
ceremony took place on September 30, 2026.
• AST operates a facility of approximately 70,000 m² in the vicinity of Jurong Lake

District and employs more than 200 highly skilled engineers and technicians.
• Focus on high-demand, large-size, high-layer-count substrates
• While the investment size is not disclosed directly by the company, Nikkei sized
the investment at around ¥50 billion (~US$317mn).
Daeduck (353200.KS, Not Covered)
Daeduck announced on July 20, 2026, that it is investing ₩497 billion (~US$370M)
manufacturing facilities and related infrastructure for semiconductor products, running

through December 31, 2027.
ABF capex is stepping up, but announced investment still falls short of implied
demand. We estimate that ABF substrate makers have announced ~US$14.3bn of
investment year to date, materially above the segment’s aggregate annual capex in recent
years, although these commitments span multiple years. Our tally is not exhaustive. LG
Innotek has yet to disclose the size of its planned ABF capacity investment, while Shinko
has not quantified the additional spending required to expand capacity following its

announced acquisition of a JDI fab.
Our supply chain checks suggest that each US$1 of investment can support US$0.50–
0.60 of annual revenue at full ramp. On this basis, the US$14.3bn announced so far could
support US$7.2–8.6bn of incremental annual revenue. This compares with US$11.4bn of
incremental revenue implied by our industry forecast. We model the ABF substrate
market reaching US$20.9bn in 2030, up from US$9.5bn in 2026. Despite the step-up in

meet our demand forecast, pointing to a need for further capacity expansion over the
coming years.

The State of the Current Cycle
The ABF substrate market has gone through a few cycles over the past two
decades. We believe we are now still in the early innings of an up-cycle.
There was a prolonged down-cycle from 2012-18 as the PC TAM shrank amid the rise of
smartphones and tablets. During this tough period, companies were not able to generate
profits and had to compete on price to fill their idle capacity. As a result, during those

years, there was no or very limited capacity expansion, and capital expenditures by the
major ABF substrate suppliers were kept at a minimum.
Even as general server demand picked up, lifting utilization rates across the group going
into 2020, ABF substrate suppliers remained cautious on the longer-term demand outlook
and remained disciplined, hesitating to expand capacity. Hence, capex turned up just
slightly in 2019-20, increasing ~US$600mn vs. ~US$2.2bn on average per year in 2012-18.

This underinvestment led to sudden undersupply, as the shock from Covid-19 drove strong
work-from-home demand for PCs, as well as increased data usage and storage for general
computing driven by online entertainment. This ultimately led to a significant increase in
ABF substrate market value from 2020-22, reflected by the sudden surge in demand
along with meaningful pricing adjustments owing to the lack of supply.
During this 2020-22 period, much capacity expansion was set in motion as ABF substrate

suppliers and customers came to agree that necessary investments were needed for
future growth. Some subsidies were even given to these suppliers to facilitate this
capacity expansion. This is why capex jumped from ~US$2.9bn in 2020 to US$4.1bn in
2021 and US$5.5bn in 2022; even in 2023-24, capex remained elevated, as capacity
expansion plans continued.
Unfortunately, demand did not play out as expected. As the world came out of the
pandemic, demand for PCs and online entertainment fell sharply. This normalization of

demand, coupled with capacity expansion from practically every major ABF substrate
supplier, resulted in the ABF substrate market once again becoming oversupplied. This led
to a sharp decline in ABF substrate market value in 2023-24, as volumes normalized,
driving prices down to normalize against the new supply/demand dynamic.
Fortunately, cloud demand remained strong for general computing, and with AI demand
starting to pick up, the down-cycle this time around was much shorter (two years) vs. the

prior down-cycle (six years).
In 1H24, the ABF substrate market troughed and we saw an upward inflection since then,
but the pace of the recovery has been moderate and bifurcated. Midrange/low-end
markets such as PCs have shown moderate demand recovery, with high-end server and AI-
related end-demand recovering and growing at a faster pace. This is why we turned the
page on our bearish stance in 1H25, but did not turn more bullish until early 2026.

### Page 14

M
Idea
14
Periods Description
Period I (2010-2012): PC-centric
equilibrium
ABF substrate demand is primarily driven and dominated by CPU and GPU demand for desktops, notebooks,
discrete GPUs, with a meaningfully smaller exposure towards server CPUs.
Period II (2012-16): PC decline
and demand dilution
Global PC demand entered  a structural down-cycle as consumer behavior changed, with smartphones and tablets
absorbing more consumer time spent. This resulted in lower volumes and increased pricing competition as

suppliers fought to fill idle capacity.
Period III (2016-18): The quiet
inflection - servers and early
HPC
During these years, the PC market matured, while cloud computing started to take off. At the same time, server
CPU substrates quietly grew larger with more layers. We also started to see early accelerators being adopted in
high performance computing.
Period IV (2018-2022): WFH
demand driven structural
shortage
The Covid-19 pandemic drove massive work from home demand for consumer electronics and cloud computing.

This sudden surge in PC demand and increased time spent on gaming and online entertainment, bundled with a
continuous increase in substrate package size and layer count, resulted in a structural shortage, which also resulted
in longer lead times and raised prices. It was during this period (2021-22) that ABF suppliers globally started to
invest in new capacity.
Period V (2022-2024): Post-
pandemic correction and new
capacity arrival
As the world came out of the Covid-19 pandemic, WFH demand faded and  PC and cloud computing demand were

negatively affected. But at the same time, the new investments and capacity additions were just starting to slowly
come online, resulting in a drop in utilization rates and pricing.
Period VI (2024-2026):
Bifurcated recovery
PC demand normalized at the pre-Covid levels, with some replacement demand driven by Windows upgrade, while
the recovery and growth of AI and general server demand for cloud picks up at a meaningfully higher growth
trajectory.

Period VII (2026-30): AI diffusion Demand for AI proliferates from training to inference, driving ABF substrate market growth to re-accelerate over the
next five years after a two-year down-cycle following the Covid-19 pandemic. This drives increased demand for AI
computing, networking, and cloud computing, while demand for PCs remains soft.

Breaking down ABF substrate end demand
The end market mix for ABF substrates has shifted dramatically over the past decade. PCs

were once the dominant demand driver, accounting for approximately 70% of global ABF
substrate market value in 2015, supported primarily by PC CPUs and GPUs. Since then,
however, the industry's demand profile has undergone a structural transformation. We
estimate that PC-related applications represented only ~23% of total ABF substrate
market value in 2025, and this share is expected to decline further to below 10% by 2030.
In contrast, demand from servers, AI GPUs, AI ASICs, and networking applications has

expanded rapidly. We estimate that these segments collectively accounted for only
around 10% of the ABF substrate market in 2015, but their share increased to ~60% by
2025. Looking ahead, we expect they will represent more than 80% of total ABF
substrate market value by 2030.
In our view, this structural shift in end-market demand has been the primary driver of ABF
substrate industry growth over the past decade and will become even more pronounced

over the next five years. As AI infrastructure deployment accelerates, we expect continued
expansion in server, AI accelerator, and networking demand to drive meaningful
acceleration in ABF substrate market growth.

Price Target and Valuation Methodology
Our 12-month price target increases ~21% to NT$1,775 (from NT$1,465), driven by our
increased earnings estimates for CY26-28: We believe the company's earnings have
returned to a growth track from 2026 onward and will show stronger  growth in 2027,
driven by the high-end ABF substrate market's reversion to undersupply and Unimicron's
dominant supply share in AI ASICs and also server CPUs, which puts it in a favorable

position to benefit from rising AI investments.
We believe the overall ABF substrate market bottomed in 2H24, and see growth
accelerating from 2H26 onward with the ramp of multiple next-generation, large body size
ABF substrates for new server GPUs, CPUs, ASICs, and networking chips. New
technologies such as embedded passives and Intel's EMIB-T should also increase the
manufacturing complexity of the package substrates, which adds to the under-capacity as

usable output decreases.
Similar to our analysis of other tech hardware companies within our coverage, we use a
residual income (RI) valuation model to value Unimicron – we think it derives the most
accurate value of the firm as it takes into account cost of equity. Our key assumptions
include:
• Cost of equity of 9.2% (unchanged)
° Risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield);
° Equity risk premium of 8.7%;
° Beta of 1.0;
• Medium-term growth rate of 14% (slightly lowered from 15%)

• Terminal growth rate of 3% (unchanged, similar to the rest of the companies under
our tech hardware coverage).
Our bull and bear case values increase by similar magnitudes, to NT$3,030 and NT
$595, respectively.

Price Target and Valuation Methodology
Our price target increases ~29%, to NT$2,000, reflecting our raised earnings
estimates for CY26-28: We believe the company's earnings have returned to a growth
track from 2026 onward and will show stronger growth in 2027, driven by the high-end
ABF substrate market's reversion to undersupply, NYPCB's dominant supply share for
networking chips, and its proactive pricing strategy, which puts it in a favorable position to

benefit from rising AI investments.
We believe the overall ABF substrate market bottomed in 2H24, and growth should
accelerate from 2H26 onward with the ramp of multiple next-generation, large body size
ABF substrates for new server GPUs, CPUs, ASICs and networking chips. New
technologies, such as embedded passives and Intel's EMIB-T, should also increase
manufacturing complexity of the package substrates, adding to the overall undercapacity
as usable output decreases.

Similar to our analysis of other tech hardware companies within our coverage, we use a
residual income (RI) valuation model to value NYPCB – we think it derives the most
accurate value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. Key assumptions
(all unchanged):
• Cost of equity of 9.3%;
° Risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield);
° Equity risk premium of 8.7%;
° Beta of 1.0;
• Medium-term growth rate of 17%  –  fair given our analysis of the substrate market

over the next couple of years;
• Terminal growth rate of 3% (similar to the rest of the companies under our tech
hardware coverage).
Our bull and bear case values increase by similar magnitudes, to NT$3,575 and NT
$1,020, respectively.

Risk Reward - Unimicron (3037.TW)
NT$1,775.00
Base case, residual income (RI) valuation model, which we think derives the most accurate
value of the ﬁrm given that it takes into account cost of equity. We use a cost of equity of
9.2% [risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of
8.7%, and a beta of 1.0], a medium-term growth rate of 14%, and a terminal growth rate of
3%.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

## Document 156: 3443 Nomura20261019

Source PDF: all report pdf/3443 Nomura20261019.pdf

### Page 1

EQUITY: FABLESS
Global Unichip Corp   3443.TW 3443 TT
Global Markets Research
19 October 2026
Riding the next megatrend of SoIC; maintain Buy
Several key CSP projects in the pipeline to support growth until 2028-29F
Benefiting from SoIC — the next megatrend in advanced packaging, in our view
GUC shares have rallied ~100% since late July (vs the TAIEX up 11%), driven by a strong 
wave of consensus earnings upward revisions (Fig. 11). This is largely in line with our 
observations highlighted in our 2 Aug report that “consensus expectations were way too low.” 
We believe the Street is starting to acknowledge that there is a strong pipeline of CSP projects 
over the next 2-3 years, including Google CPU (GOOGL US, Not rated; revenue sharply 
higher in 2026 with momentum potentially sustaining into 2027F and 2028F as Axion 3 
doubles silicon content from Axion 2), as well as the other top three US hyperscalers’ ASIC 
and CPU projects along with Tesla’s (TSLA US, Not rated) AI5/AI6.5. GUC not only has 
secured projects from top US hyperscalers, but at the same time acquired additional 
meaningful customers with volume globally, e.g. China auto ASIC and US AI ASIC using a 
SoW approach. However, among all, the one that has caught our attention the most is the 
Google TPU v.10 compute die opportunity (though we believe the turnkey deal is yet to be 
finalized) — not only because the addressable size could be significant, but this business 
opportunity might originate from GUC’s G-link 3D die-on-die IP. (Fig. 1 Fig. 4) We raise our 
2026-28F EPS for GUC by 15%-37% to incorporate business upside from non-Axion projects, 
and raise TP to TWD9,600 (from TWD5,800), based on an unchanged 40x target P/E, while 
rolling forward valuation to the 2027-28F average EPS. We are yet to incorporate overseas 
convertible bonds (ECB) in our model/EPS estimates.
The rising importance of SoIC in advanced packaging should benefit GUC, given its 
leading investments in Glink-3D die-on-die IP among design service peers
SoIC is not new, but it was small before 2026 given limited adoption by AMD MI300 chip (AMD 
US, Not rated) and Apple M-series chip (AAPL US, Not rated). In 2026, nVidia’s (NVDA US, Not 
rated) demand for CPO using TSMC’s (2330 TT, Buy) COUPE (EIC on PIC stacking) has further 
lifted SoIC demand. In our June anchor report, we noted that nVidia’s Feynman could become 
the first GPU-on-GPU stacking SoIC (along with SiC carrier) AI chip. Since then, several US CSP 
ASIC projects have also planned to enable SoIC die stacking in their chip roadmap, according to 
our industry checks. GUC’s Glink-3D IP (see Fig. 1- Fig. 4) appears well positioned to ride this 
trend as it has developed this IP along with TSMC’s roadmap and currently a number of CSPs 
are using its IP to build their test chip – which in the long run could prove to be a good stepping 
stone for GUC to win more services, in our view. Besides, GUC’s SoIC IP would also be 
deployed with TSMC’s scale-up CPO customers (e.g. AyarLabs, Fig. 5).

### Page 3

Company profile
Established in 1998, GUC is one of the leading design service companies in Taiwan with self-developed IPs. TSMC is GUC's single-largest
shareholder with a 35% share.
Valuation Methodology
Our TP of TWD9,600 is based on 40x 2027-28F average EPS (40x P/E is its historical mid level). The benchmark index for the stock is TAIEX.
Risks that may impede the achievement of the target price
Downside risks include 1) fierce ASIC competition, 2) slowdown of key projects and lack of capacity, and 3) more insourcing trend from customers

ESG
GUC has six major ESG strategies to achieve the goals of sustainable development, including corporate governance, innovative R&D, partnership
prosperity, environmental sustainability, friendly workplace, and social participation.

Valuation methodology and risks
Our TP of TWD9,600 is based on 40x 2027-28F average EPS (40x P/E is its historical 
mid level). The benchmark index for the stock is the TAIEX.
Downside risks include: (1) fierce ASIC competition, (2) slowdown of key projects and 
lack of capacity, and (3) increased in-sourcing from customers.

Definition of Nomura Group's equity research rating system and sectors
The rating system is a relative system, indicating expected performance against a specific benchmark identified for each individual stock, 
subject to limited management discretion. An analyst’s target price is an assessment of the current intrinsic fair value of the stock based on an 
appropriate valuation methodology determined by the analyst. Valuation methodologies include, but are not limited to, discounted cash flow 
analysis, expected return on equity and multiple analysis. Analysts may also indicate expected absolute upside/downside relative to the stated 
target price, defined as (target price - current price)/current price.

SECTORS
A 'Bullish' stance, indicates that the analyst expects the sector to outperform the Benchmark during the next 12 months. A 'Neutral' stance, 
indicates that the analyst expects the sector to perform in line with the Benchmark during the next 12 months. A 'Bearish' stance, indicates that 
the analyst expects the sector to underperform the Benchmark during the next 12 months. Sectors that are labelled as 'Not rated' or shown as 
'N/A' are not assigned ratings. Benchmarks are as follows: United States: S&P 500; Europe: Dow Jones STOXX 600; Global Emerging 
Markets (ex-Asia): MSCI Emerging Markets ex-Asia. Japan/Asia ex-Japan: Sector ratings are not assigned.

Target Price
A Target Price, if discussed, indicates the analyst’s forecast for the share price with a 12-month time horizon, reflecting in part the analyst's 
estimates for the company's earnings. The achievement of any target price may be impeded by general market and macroeconomic trends, and 
by other risks related to the company or the market, and may not occur if the company's earnings differ from estimates.

後 100% 防 復 發 數 據 ， 中 長 期 佈 局 完 整：  
原 發 性 血 小 板 過 多 症(ET) 為 過 去 30 年 來 缺 乏 新 藥 之 領 域，公 司

個股報告

2 群益投顧 
  ESG ：  
因 應 企 業 永 續 經 營 所 帶 來 的 挑 戰 與 機 會，整 合 各 方 利 害 關 係 人 關 注 之 議 題，
將 展 開 「 永 續 經 營(ESG) 計 畫 」 ， 成 立 企 業 永 續 發 展 委 員 會 ， 聚 焦 於 五 大 策
略 ， 連 結 聯 合 國 「 永 續 發 展 目 標 SDGs 」 ， 以 企 業 核 心 精 神 與 專 業 ， 落 實 永
續 經 營 各 項 目 標，促 進 永 續 的 經 濟 成 長、社 會 發 展 與 環 境 保 護，提 升 企 業 競
爭 力 與 發 揮 影 響 力 。 
公 司 簡 介 ： 
藥華藥主要致力於研發和生產新一代創新型生物藥，特別聚焦於血液腫瘤
領 域 。 公 司 旗 艦 產 品 為 Ropeginterferon alfa -2b ( 簡稱 Ropeg ，商品名
Besremi) ， 是 一 個 創 新 的 長 效 型 干 擾 素 藥 物 ， 目 前 已 在 美 國 、 歐 洲 、 日 本 、
韓 國 、 台 灣 及 中 國 等 全 球 約 50 個 國 家 取 得 藥 證 ， 用 於 治 療 成 人 真 性 紅 血 球
增多症 (Polycythemia Vera, PV) ， 並 已 陸 續 於 台 灣 、 日 本 及 美 國 取 得 原 發 性
血 小 板 過 多 症(ET) 藥 證 。  
Ropeg 是 藥 華 藥 自 行 研 發、並 擁 有 全 球 獨 家 權 利 的 新 藥。公 司 利 用 其 特 有 的
長 效 型 蛋 白 質 藥 物 技 術 平 台 ， 成 功 開 發 出Ropeg 。 過 去 PV 的 治 療 藥 物 有 限
且 常 伴 隨 副 作 用 ， 包 含 老 藥 HU 及 尚 未 獲 PV 適 應 症 批 准 的 干 擾 素 藥 物 ， 與
美 國 藥 廠 Incyte 開 發 的 Jakafi (ruxolitinib) 。 2019 年， Ropeg 獲 得 歐 洲 藥 品
管理局 (EMA) 核 准 上 市 ， 並 在 2021 年 11 月 獲 得 美 國 FDA 的 核 准 ， 成 為 第
一個被 FDA 批 准 用 於 所 有 PV 成 人 的 治 療 藥 物 ， 不 受 之 前 治 療 史 的 限 制 ，
也 是 第 一 個 被 FDA 核 准 特 別 用 於 PV 的 干 擾 素 療 法 ， 且 已 連 續 四 年 獲NCCN
治 療 指 引 推 薦 。 
相 較 於 傳 統 干 擾 素 ，Ropeg 具 有 更 長 的 半 衰 期 ， 因 此 可 以 延 長 給 藥 間 隔 ， 提
高 病 患 的 便 利 性 及 順 從 性，同 時 也 展 現 出 更 優 異 的 療 效。 在 7.3 年 的 臨 床 追
蹤 數 據 顯 示 76% 的 完 全 血 液 學 反 應 率(CHR) ， 並 能 持 續 降 低 JAK2 基 因 突 變
負 擔 。 藥 華 藥 在 美 國 、 歐 洲 、 日 本 及 台 灣 等 地 設 有 子 公 司 ， 負 責 藥 物 的 銷
售 與 推 廣 。Ropeg 目 前 是 公 司 最 主 要 的 營 收 來 源 ， 佔 比 超 過9 成 。 此 外 也 積
極拓展 Ropeg 的 其 他 適 應 症 ， 包 含 血 小 板 過 多 症(ET) 及 骨 髓 纖 維 化(MF) 等。。 
3Q26 營 收 季 增，9 月 未 含 CMS 調 整 仍 創 新 高，財 務 營 運 槓 桿 與 獲 利 結 構 持
續 優 化 ：  
2Q2 6 營收 68. 55 億元 (YoY+90.30%) ， 主 要 源 於 美 國 與 日 本 兩 大 高 價 值 市 場
銷 量 顯 著 成 長 ， 團 隊 擴 編 與 精 準 醫 學 行 銷 策 略 效 益 大 舉 顯 現 。 毛 利 率
94.2 3%， 受 惠 美 國 5M2 6 調 升 藥 價，以及 CMS 回沖， 營 業 費 用 33.00 億元
(YoY +60.68%) ， 增 加 主 因 延 續 團 隊 擴 編 、 商 業 推 廣 以 及 依 會 計 準 則 認 列 限
制 員 工 權 利 新 股 約 2 億多元 ，增 幅 遠 低 於 營 收 成 長，展 現 高 營 運 槓 桿 之 強 勁
爆發力 。  
ET 新 適 應 症 在 全 球 步 入 收 割 期 ， 併 購 予 宇 切 入 新 興 療 法 ， 加 上PMF 收案
順 利 與 肝 癌 術 後 100% 防 復 發 數 據 ， 中 長 期 佈 局 完 整：  
原 發 性 血 小 板 過 多 症(ET) 為過去 30 年 來 缺 乏 新 藥 之 領 域 ， 公 司 於05/12/2026
率 先 取 得 台 灣 ET 藥 證 ， 用 於 HU 抗 藥 性 或 無 法 耐 受 之 成 人 ET 病 人 ； 日 本
MHLW 專 家 會 議 於 07/2026 通 過 審 查 ， 破 例 同 意 將 全 球 三 期 SURPASS -ET
與 北 美 單 臂 二 期 XC 數 據 合 併 評 估 ， 並 已 於 08/24/2026 正 式 獲 日 本 核 准 用

3 群益投顧 
於 所 有 成 人 ET 患 者 ， 包 括 未 曾 接 受 治 療 者 ； 隨 後 美 國FDA 亦 正 式 核 准 Ropeg
用 於 所 有 成 人 ET 患 者 ， 成 為 近 30 年首款 ET 新 藥 ， 並 具 7 年 孤 兒 藥 獨 佔
期，配 合 芝 加 哥 誓 師 大 會 已 完 成 之 團 隊 培 訓 與 推 廣 文 宣，取 證 後 已 立 即 展 開
商 業 推 廣 ； 其 他 市 場 方 面 ， 中 國 大 陸 與 韓 國ET 審 查 同 步 進 行 ， 分 別 預 計 於
11/2026 及 12/2026 取 證 ， 同 時 準 備 於 澳 洲 啟 動 PV 及 ET 雙 適 應 症 藥 證 申
請。早 期 原 發 性 骨 髓 纖 維 化(PMF) 三 期 臨 床 HOPE -PMF 於 06/2026 完 成 收 案
且 超 收 至 160 人 (原目標 150 人 )， 預 計 2027 年 完 成 試 驗 、2028 年 中 申 請 藥
證。  
在 實 體 腫 瘤 領 域 ，B 型 肝 炎 相 關 肝 癌(HCC) 術 後 防 復 發 一 期 試 驗 ，Ropeg 序
列合併 Anti -PD1 在 15 名 患 者 中 位 追 蹤 1,024 天 ， 創 下 100% 無 復 發 存 活 率
(RFS) 之 重 大 突 破 。PEG -IL -2 (P11838) 、 PD -1-IL -2v 、 ADC 及 KRAS TCR -T
將於 2026 -2027 年 陸 續 申 請 IND 。  
藥 華 藥 近 期 斥 資 約 1.68 億 元 收 購 予 宇 生 技 100% 股 權 ， 取 得 脂 質 奈 米 顆 粒
(LNP) 藥 物 遞 送 平 台 、 研 發 設 備 及 專 業 團 隊 ， 並 納 入 兩 項 新 藥 研 發 專 案 。 予
宇成立於 2019 年， LNP 平 台 涵 蓋 配 方 設 計 、 微 流 體 製 程 與 分 析 方 法 ， 可 支
援 mRNA 、 saRNA 及 circRNA 等 多 元 RNA 藥 物 開 發 ； 原 有 FR -202 與 FR -
201 兩 項 專 案 分 別 鎖 定 端 粒 生 物 學 疾 病 與 威 爾 森 氏 症 ， 預 計 於 2027 年及
2028 年 提 出 IND 。  
此 項 收 購 對 公 司 營 運 具 三 大 正 面 意 義 ：(1) 收 購 金 額 僅 約 1.68 億 元 ， 相 對 公
司單季逾 25 億 元 淨 利 規 模 ， 財 務 負 擔 極 低 ；(2) 取 得 遞 送 系 統 自 主 能 力 ， 可
與公司既有蛋白質藥物製造資源整合，從研發早期即同步規劃藥物設計與
後 續 製 造 ；(3) 延 伸 研 發 版 圖 至 In Vivo CAR -T 此 一 國 際 大 廠 積 極 布 局 的 領
域 ， 提 升 中 長 期 選 擇 權 價 值 ， 亦 為 後 續 研 發 產 品 線 布 局 增 加 深 度 與 廣 度 。 
注 射 筆 上 市 與 潛 力 患 者 數 上 修 雙 輪 驅 動，配 合 真 實 世 界 數 據 防止 惡 化，鞏 固
Ropeg 在 PV 領 域 之 地 位 ：  
Ropeg 在 PV 適 應 症 已 於 全 球 約 50 個 國 家 獲 批 ， 加 拿 大 亦 於 07/02/2026 
順 利 取 證 。 美 國 FDA 於 06/2026 核准 Ropeg 注 射 筆 ， 並 於 07/2026 正 式 出
貨 開 賣，注 射 筆 具 備 極 細 針 頭、精 準 調 劑 及 一 次 性 自 動 縮 回 防 針 扎 設 計，短
期 可 加 速 替 換 傳 統 預 充 充 填 針 筒 Prefilled Syringe ， 中 長 期 將 提 升 患 者 長 期
用 藥 依 從 性 與 留 存 率 。 
武田製藥 (Takeda) 競品 Rusfertide (擬 似 鐵 調 素)預 計 於 08/2026 獲 批，藥 華 藥
指出  Rusfertide 僅 定 位 於 配 合 放 血 之 被 動 症 狀 控 制 ， 而 Ropeg 為 一 線 首 選
積 極 療 法 ， 真 實 世 界 數 據 證 實 Ropeg 合 併 治 療 可 使 患 者 疾 病 惡 化成 骨 髓 纖
維化  (MF )的 概 率 維 持 在 0%。最 新 流 行 病 學 文 獻 將 美 國 MPN 總 潛 力 患 者 數
上修至 34.70 萬人 ( 原 30.50 萬人 )， 競 品 進 入市 場 將 大 幅 提 升 早 期 治 療 之 病
識 感 與 診 斷 率 ， 反 而 有 助 於 Ropeg 進 一 步 擴 大 一 線 市 場 壟 斷 地 位 。 
藥 華 藥 與 AOP 仲 裁 案 ： 
關於 AOP 第 二 階 段 仲 裁 判 斷 ， 藥 華 藥 認 為 其 程 序 存 在 違 反 程 序 正 義 及 聽 審
權 之 重 大 瑕 疵 ， 目 前 正 與 外 部 律 師 團 隊 嚴 謹 評 估 ， 將 在 法 定 時 限 內 向 德 國
法 蘭 克 福 高 等 法 院 提 起 「 撤 銷 第 二 階 段 仲 裁 判 斷 之 訴 」 。 
由 於 法 律 程 序 仍 在 評 估 中 ， 現 階 段 無 法 判 斷 或 預 測 該 裁 定 金 額 發 生 的 時 間
點 ， 後 續 發 展 視 訴 訟 進 度 而 定 。 公 司 與AOP 之 合 約 目 前 仍 處 於 有 效 狀 態 ，

對 於 任 何 有 助 於 妥 善 解 決 爭 議 、 維 護 股 東 權 益 之 方 案 均 持 開 放 態 度 。 歐 洲

定 價 之 認 定)的 計 算 邏 輯 套 用 至 2023 -2025 年供貨， AOP 主 張 藥 華 藥 需 再 退
還約 €65M ， 此 項 目 僅 為 AOP 單 方 面 之 延 伸 主 張 ， 非 現 階 段 確 定 之 仲 裁 判
決。

性認列約 36 億 元 損 失 衝 擊 ， 不 至 於 構 成 營 運 生 存 危 機。

率 ， 推 升 低 風 險 PV/ET 患 者 滲 透 率 ， 為 2H26 營 收 加 速 引 擎 ；(2)ET 新 適 應
症 迎 來 多 國 藥 證 收 割，開 啟 第 二 成 長 曲 線，台 灣、日本 取 證 後，美國 FDA 亦

7 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15 %

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 ， 亦 將 出 具 降 評 報 告 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

## Document 191: 9月ＦＯＭＣ議事録：タカ派姿勢に滲む「データ依存」の慎重さ ～FRBは年内追加利上げを視野に入れるも、決定は今後の経済指標次第～

Source PDF: all report pdf/9月ＦＯＭＣ議事録：タカ派姿勢に滲む「データ依存」の慎重さ   ～FRBは年内追加利上げを視野に入れるも、決定は今後の経済指標次第～.pdf

FED  Watching

2026 年 10 月 8 日

1/5 
9 月ＦＯＭＣ議事録：タカ派姿勢に滲む「データ依存」の慎重さ 
～FRB は年内追加利上げを視野に入れるも、決定は今後の経済指標次第～ 
 経済調査部 主任エコノミスト  桂畑 誠治  
（℡：050-5474-7493）

要旨  
・9 月FOMCでは全会一致（12 対 0）で 0.25%の利上げを決定。非投票メンバーを含む参加者 
全 19 人が利上げに賛成した。 
・経済・金利見通し（SEP）提出者の大半が年内（10 月または 12 月）の更なる利上げを見込 
んでおり、全体としてタカ派的トーンが際立つ。 
・インフレリスクは上振れ傾向にあるものの、中長期のインフレ予想は 2%目標水準に安定。 
FRBは毎会合のデータ次第で柔軟に判断する「オープンな姿勢」を維持している。 
・今後の見通しとしては、景気や労働市場が安定する中でコアCPIやコアPCEの鈍化が確認され 
れば、予防的な追加利上げが見送られる可能性がある（最終決定は 12 月FOMCまでのデータ次
第） 。

１． 9 月FOMC ：参加者 19 人全員 が利上げ に賛成  
ケビン・ウォーシュFRB議長の新体制下で初めての利上げが決定された 9 月FOMCの議事録が公表 
された。新体制では会合直後の声明文のテキスト量が大幅に削減されたほか、記者会見時間も短
縮され、直接的な情報発信量が絞り込まれている。そうした中で、後日公表される「議事録」の
情報量は引き続き高水準に維持されており、FRBの本音を推し量る最重要ツールとしての重みが増
している。ただし、公表がFOMC終了から 3 週間後となるため、情報の遅効性には注意が必要であ 
る。 
9 月の利上げ決定については、投票権のないメンバーも含めた参加者全員（19 人）が利上げに賛
成した。さらに、経済・金利予測（SEP：議長自身は今回も SEP を提出していない）で示されていた 
通り、SEP を提出した 18 人の参加者のうち大半が「年内（10 月 27-28 日または 12 月 15-16 日）に
更なる利上げが適切となる可能性が高い」と見解を示しており、インフレへの強い警戒感を示すタ 
カ派（金融引き締め寄り）なトーンが際立つものとなった。ただし、委員会として今後の政策判断 
は新たに入手されるデータに依存（Data-dependent）するスタンスを明確に維持している。

２．労働市場は完全雇用水準で安定、インフレには上振れリスク 
委員会は全会一致（12 対 0）で、FF金利誘導目標レンジを 0.25％ポイント引き上げ、3.75％〜

### Page 2

2/5 
4.00％とすることを決定した。労働市場が完全雇用に近い水準で安定している一方で、インフレ 
率が高止まりしていることが背景にある。 
インフレの判断では、参加者は共通して、 「インフレ率は高止まりしており、ここ数ヶ月でイン 
フレ抑制に向けた十分な進展は見られていない」と指摘した。 「原油や精製燃料製品の価格を押し 
上げた継続的な地政学的動向や、急増するAI関連投資がインフレ圧力に寄与している」との認識 
が示された。 
複数の参加者がコア財・コアサービス価格の上振れや、関税の影響に言及したほか、数名の参 
加者がPCE指数の算出手法改定による影響や、3 ヶ月年率換算のインフレ率が年後半にインフレを 
過小評価しやすい傾向に留意すべきだと警鐘を鳴らした。一方で参加者全員が、 「中長期のインフ 
レ予想に関する市場ベースおよび調査ベースの指標が、委員会の 2％目標と一致する水準を維持し 
ている」と判断しており、追加利上げを過度に急ぐ必要性は示されなかった。 
労働市場に関しては、参加者は概ね「労働市場の状況が安定している」と判断し、 「完全雇用に 
近い状態にある」と評価した。 「失業率が現在の水準付近にとどまり、労働市場の状況は引き続き 
安定する」と予想した。一方で、数名の参加者が低水準の採用・解雇率など流動性の低下を指摘 
し、一部の参加者がAI専門職における局所的な賃金上昇圧力に触れた。そのうえで、参加者は概
ね「労働市場に対する上振れリスクと下振れリスクはほぼ均衡している」と評価した。 
経済活動に関しても、参加者は一般に「堅調なペースで拡大している」と評価。 「地政学的動向 
による供給面での悪影響が強まったものの、旺盛な設備投資と底堅い個人消費が経済活動を支え 
ている」との判断が示された。なお、一部の参加者からは、AI関連投資が設備投資と潜在生産性 
を押し上げる一方で中期的に総需要が総供給を上回るリスクや、株高などを背景とする金融環境 
の緩和的状況、高所得層と低・中所得層における消費の二極化構造への懸念が指摘された。 
以上のように、参加者は共通して「労働市場が完全雇用に近く力強さの兆候も見られる一方で 
インフレ率は高止まりしており、経済活動が堅調なペースで拡大していること」を強調した。さ
らに、ほぼすべての参加者が、 「インフレリスクは上振れに傾いているものの、労働市場に対する 
リスクは減少し、現在はほぼ均衡している」と評価した。こうした経済見通しと変化するリスク 
バランスに基づき、全参加者（19 人）がFF金利誘導目標レンジの引き上げが適切であると判断し
た。

3．政策金利の水準感とリスク管理の姿勢 
今回の議論において注目されるのは、利上げの根拠と政策金利水準に対する見解の多様さであ 
る。多くの参加者は、予想以上の需要や供給面での悪影響によりインフレ率が持続的に目標を上 
回り続けることに対する保険（Insurance）を提供するため、誘導目標レンジを高めの軌道とする 
ことがリスク管理上の観点から賢明であると強調した。 
一方で、相当数の参加者はリスク管理上の観点からではなく、自身の標準的な見通し（Modal 
outlooks）に基づいて高めの目標軌道が必要であると見なした。さらに、数名の参加者は中立金 
利の推計値を引き上げたことに触れ、現在の政策金利は「緊縮的ではない、あるいは極めて緩や

当化する材料となる。 
また、バランスシート政策に関しては、数名の参加者が国債市場の円滑な機能を認めつつも、 
将来の市場不全に備えたツールやコミュニケーションの強化を提案したことが確認された。

４．先行きに対する見通し 
今回の会合以降の金融政策の見通しに関して、大半の参加者（SEP提出者 18 人のうち）が年末 
までにFF金利誘導目標レンジを再度引き上げることが適切となる可能性が高いとの見方を示し 
た。ただし、全員が共通のスタンスとしてオープンな姿勢で各会合に臨み、今後の決定は新たに 
入手する情報と、それが経済見通しおよびリスクバランスに与える含意に依存することを強調、
毎会合ごとに判断されることを示し、政策の自由度を維持した。 
今回の議事録は全体として「年内追加利上げ」に前向きなタカ派的トーンが目立ったものの、 
インフレ予想の安定や「毎会合データ次第」というスタンスから、FRBが無理な連続利上げを焦っ 
ているわけではないことが示された。今後の金融政策の焦点を左右するのは、10 月・12 月FOMCま
でに発表される景気、労働市場や、コアCPI、コアPCEの動向である。景気や労働市場の安定が続 
くと予想されるもとで、インフレの鈍化傾向が確認される場合、予防的な追加利上げは見送られ 
る可能性がある（最終決定は今後のデータ次第） 。

### Page 4

経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告

りません。

ECO CHARTS | BUSINESS CREATIONS AND BANKRUPTCIES IS A NEW PUBLIC

-

ECONOMIC STUDIES
•
Business
creations
rose
by
12
%
y/y
in
the

eight

of
2026
,

an

compared

in
2025
(+

.
3
%
)
.
•

was

and

where
the

is

.
•
Meanwhile,
business
bankruptcies

rose
by
5
.
3
%
y/y
from

to

data
from

de
France
(vs
+
3
.
6
%
y/y
in
2025
)
.
After

rise
in

first
quarter
(+
8
%
y/y),
this

has
since
slowed
.

could

12
.
3

2026
(
14
.
1

earlier),

average
of
13
.
1
days
.
•
The

in
business

is

in

business

.

,

3
%
.

.

-

.
0

.

.

Banque de France
Monthly
Total outstanding bank loans to newly bankrupt NFCs over a one-year period, calculated as the figure for

the month in which the firm is declared bankrupt, relative to the average outstanding bank loans to both
healthy and bankrupt NFCs during the same period.

VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tu ần qua, -30.55 điểm tương ứng -1.68%, với thanh

[Cập nhật công ty]

[Nhận định xu hướng] 
Áp lực điều chỉnh vẫn có thể còn tiếp diễn, tuy nhiên khi xu hướng tăng ngắn hạn 
chưa bị phá vỡ, VNINDEX đư ợc kỳ vọng sẽ nhận được lực đỡ tại vùng hỗ trợ gần 
quanh 1750, tương ứng vùng hợp lưu quanh SMA50, và có cơ hội hồi phục trở lại

### Page 2

2

Điểm nhấn thị trường 
Chỉ số, nhóm ngành, khối ngoại và phái sinh

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -30.55 điểm tương ứng -1.68%, với thanh khoản suy 
giảm. Dệt may, Tiện ích khí và Bao bì & đóng gói là các nhóm ngành có mức giảm rõ rệt 
nhất trong tuần. Khối ngoại đảo chiều bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với giá trị  
-2,865 tỷ đồng, tập trung ở các mã VHM, VPB và VIC; đồng thời, tự doanh đảo chiều bán 
ròng ở mức -146 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại bán ròng -3,351 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần qua nhưng 
đang duy trì vị thế mua lũy kế 1,650 hợp đồng. Tự doanh mua ròng 2,822 hợp đồng nhưng 
đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -6,636 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam bước vào tuần đầu tiên ở vị thế thị trường mới nổi bằng một nhịp điều 
chỉnh, khi VN-Index để mất mốc 1.800 điểm và lùi về đóng cửa quanh 1.785 điểm. Đây là 
phản ứng "mua kỳ vọng, bán thực tế" khá điển hình: ngay trong phiên nâng hạng chính 
thức 21/9, chỉ số mở cửa hưng phấn rồi nhanh chóng đảo chiều do áp lực chốt lời, với 
nhóm cổ phiếu Vingroup (VIC, VHM, VRE) giảm mạnh dẫn dắt đà đi xuống, và sức ép lan 
rộng sang bất động sản, ngân hàng, năng lượng về cuối tuần. Điểm đáng chú ý là dòng 
vốn ngoại đã tiếp tục bán ròng ngay sau cột mốc ngày 21/9, dù đã mua ròng trở lại trong 
tuần trước đó. Bối cảnh bên ngoài cũng kém thuận lợi khi Fed vừa tăng lãi suất và phát đi 
tín hiệu diều hâu, chứng khoán Mỹ điều chỉnh, giá dầu neo cao, và lợi suất trái phiếu 
Chính phủ Mỹ tăng vọt. Trong một, hai tuần tới, bên cạnh các yếu tố ngoại biên, mùa báo 
cáo KQKD quý 3 cũng sẽ bắt đầu tác động lên diễn biến thị trường chung.

Lợi nhuận sau thuế công ty mẹ tăng 
trưởng khả quan trong 2Q26 14% 
YoY, với động lực chính đến từ mảng 
dịch vụ CNTT nước ngoài

Mảng CNTT nước ngoài: Lĩnh vực dịch 
vụ IT phân cực mạnh mẽ do tác nhân 
AI (AI Agentic) s ẽ củng c ố xu hư ớng 
đầu tư vào AI

FPT ở vị thế thuận lợi cho phép 
doanh nghiệp đa dạng hóa công cụ 
AI và tối ưu chi phí vận hành

Khuyến ngh ị MUA với giá m ục tiêu 
86,000 VND/cổ phiếu 
 Do không còn h ạch toán FPT Telecom (FOX) là công ty con, d oanh thu 2Q2026 của 
FPT giảm mạnh còn 13,810 tỷ VND (-17% YoY). Lợi nhuận sau thuế công ty mẹ không 
bị ảnh hưởng bởi thay đổi hạch toán đạt 2,568 tỷ VND (14% YoY). Mảng dịch vụ CNTT 
tại thị trường nước ngoài tiếp tục là đ ộng lực tăng trư ởng chính, đạt doanh thu và 
LNTT lần lượt là 9,868/1,538 tỷ VND (16.3% /19.3% YoY).

Sự chuyển dịch chi tiêu từ dịch vụ IT truyền thống sang chuyển đổi số và tích hợp AI 
được kỳ vọng sẽ diễn ra ngày m ột mạnh mẽ khi: i) Sự phát triển của năng l ực tính 
toán giúp kéo giảm chi phí token (đơn v ị năng lực tính toán của AI) và thúc đ ẩy nhu 
cầu sử dụng AI của doanh nghiệp; ii) Các mô hình AI mã nguồn mở gia tăng lựa chọn 
cho người dùng sẽ thúc đẩy chi tiêu

Đối với các doanh nghiệp ứng dụng sâu các mô hình AI phục vụ hoạt động kinh doanh 
như FPT , chúng tôi nhận thấy xu hướng tối ưu chi phí tốt hơn nhờ khả năng sử dụng 
các mô hình AI đ ến từ đa dạng nhà cung c ấp; với chi phí trên hi ệu năng tính toán 
được cải thi ện. Trong k ịch b ản cơ s ở, chúng tôi d ự báo biên l ợi nhuận trước thu ế 
mảng CNTT nước ngoài sẽ ổn định ở mức 15.4% nhờ duy trì chi ến lược quản trị chi 
phí hiệu quả qua đó giúp LNTT 2026/2027 đạt 6,198/7,087 tỷ VND (13.3%/14.3% YoY).

Dựa trên định giá FCFF và P/E, triển vọng kinh doanh cũng như xem xét các rủi ro có 
thể phát sinh, chúng tôi khuyến nghị MUA đối với cổ phiếu FPT . Giá mục tiêu là 86,000 
VND/cổ phiếu.

Các mảng kinh doanh duy trì ổn định 
giúp lợi nhuận sau thuế công ty mẹ 
tăng trưởng nhẹ

Sản lượng điện của REE tăng trưởng 
nhẹ nhờ mức tăng của mảng điện gió 
sau khi dự án Duyên Hải V1-4 vận 
hành

Mảng M&E ghi nhận giá trị ký mới cao 
trong 1H.2026, t ạo kỳ vọng hồi phục 
mạnh mẽ doanh thu

Mảng cho thuê văn phòng đứng trước 
cơ hội lớn đóng góp vào tăng trư ởng 
nhờ các điều kiện thuận lợi

Khuyến nghị MUA với giá mục tiêu 
58,100 VNĐ/cổ phiếu

Doanh thu Quý 2/2026 giảm nhẹ, đạt 2,432 tỷ VND (-3% YoY/-2% QoQ), trong khi l ợi 
nhuận sau thuế công ty mẹ đạt 632 tỷ VND (+1% YoY/-11.5% QoQ), do doanh thu của 
mảng bất động sản suy giảm. Qua đó, REE hoàn thành lần lượt 40%/47.8% kế hoạch 
doanh thu và LNST công ty mẹ 2026.

Trong Q2.2026, tổng sản lượng phát đi ện của REE tăng nh ẹ, đạt 2.8 tỷ kWh (+2.3% 
YoY) với đóng góp tăng trưởng rõ nét đến từ mảng điện gió khi dự án Duyên Hải V1-4 
bắt đầu vận hành; tuy nhiên  sản lượng thủy điện suy gi ảm do tác đ ộng tiêu c ực từ 
pha thời tiết El Nino, đạt 1.1 tỷ kWh.

Với năng lực chuyên môn được nâng cao, mảng M&E ghi nhận những dấu hiệu phục 
hồi tích cực trong giá trị hợp đồng ký mới trong 1H.2026, 5,865 tỷ VND (cao hơn 53.9% 
so với giá trị ký mới cả năm 2025). KBSV dự phóng doanh thu c ủa mảng M&E sẽ đạt 
4,499/5,190 tỷ VND (+28%/15% YoY) trong 2026/2027.

Hội tụ bởi 3 yếu tố: i) Định hướng phát triển kinh tế Hồ Chí Minh trở thành trung tâm 
tài chính quốc tế; ii) Hạ tầng giao thông ngày một được hoàn thiện và iii) Chi phí thuê 
cạnh tranh so với chất lượng dịch vụ của E. Town 6, KBSV dự phóng doanh thu mảng 
cho thuê văn phòng trong 2026/2027 tăng trưởng +14%/6.2% YoY.

Dựa trên đ ịnh giá SOTP , triển vọng kinh doanh cũng như xem xét các r ủi ro có th ể

Doanh thu 2Q.26 bứt phá nhờ khoản 
thu nhập bất thường của nhiệt điện 
Cà Mau và Vũng Áng

Nhiệt đi ện than duy trì công su ất ở 
mức cao nh ờ nguồn cung nhiên li ệu 
ổn định trong 2H26

Điện khí Đông Nam Bộ (NT1&2 và 
NT3&4) có triển vọng hoạt động 
thuận lợi

Khuyến nghị MUA với giá mục tiêu 
18,100 VND/cổ phiếu 
 Trong Quý 2/2026, POW ghi nhận Doanh thu và LNST cổ đông công ty mẹ đạt lần lượt 
20,308 tỷ VND (116.1% YoY) và 3,519 tỷ VND (484.6% YoY) chủ yếu nhờ các khoản thu 
nhập từ chênh lệch tiền khí của nhiệt điện Cà Mau và tỷ giá của nhiệt điện Vũng Áng. 
Với kết qu ả trên, POW hoàn thành l ần lư ợt 65%/420% kế hoạch doanh thu và l ợi 
nhuận sau thuế công ty mẹ.

Chúng tôi cho r ằng nhà máy nhi ệt than Vũng Áng trong 2026 s ẽ được duy trì công 
suất huy động ở mức cao nhờ: i) Xác suất diễn ra El Nino tăng cao và dự kiến kéo dài 
đến đầu 2027, ii) Nguồn cung than trong nước được bảo đảm từ các đối tác (TKV, TCT 
Đông B ắc) và d ự trữ than d ồi dào c ủa doanh nghi ệp ghi nh ận ở mức cao trong 
Q2/2026.

Dự án đường dây 500kV Mạch 3 đi vào vận hành giúp giảm áp lực cạnh tranh của các 
nhà máy điện tại khu vực phía Nam, qua đó kỳ vọng doanh thu của NT1&2 trong năm 
2026 đạt 11,104 tỷ VND (5.3% YoY). Đối với dự án điện NT3&4, khoản thu nhập chênh 
lệch sản lượng theo giá công suất do sản lượng điện thực tế thấp hơn so với tỷ lệ Qc 
65% của nhà máy được duy trì sang 2H26 và 2027 sẽ giúp cho dự án ghi nhận doanh 
thu lần lượt 17,848/17,621 tỷ VND doanh thu trong 2026/2027.

Dựa trên định giá SOTP và EV/EBITDA, tri ển vọng kinh doanh cũng như xem xét các 
rủi ro có th ể phát sinh, chúng tôi khuy ến nghị MUA đối với cổ phiếu POW. Giá m ục 
tiêu là 18,100 VND/cổ phiếu.

CTCP Dịch vụ Hàng không Taseco (AST) 
Tiếp tục mở rộng điểm kinh doanh 
22/09/2026  
Chuyên viên cao cấp Nguyễn Ngọc Anh 
(+84) 24-7303-5333 anhntn@kbsec.com.vn

KQKD tăng trư ởng tích c ực trong 
2Q2026

Tăng trư ởng khách qua c ảng hàng 
không thúc đ ẩy doanh thu t ại các 
điểm kinh doanh hiện hữu

Mở rộng mạng lưới tạo động lực tăng 
trưởng doanh thu mới

Cơ c ấu kinh doanh và hi ệu qu ả vận 
hành hỗ trợ cải thiện biên lợi nhuận

Chúng tôi khuyến nghị MUA, giá mục 
tiêu 76,500 VND/cổ phiếu

2Q2026, AST ghi nh ận doanh thu và LNST đ ạt 479/92 t ỷ VND (+22%/19% yoy), với 
động lực chủ yếu đến từ (1) đóng góp từ 19 điểm kinh doanh mở mới tại T3 Tân Sơn 
Nhất và (2) tăng trưởng tích cực của các mảng hàng miễn thuế, nhà hàng - fastfood 
và phòng ch ờ thương gia. Lũy k ế 6T2026, doanh thu và LNST đ ạt 1,035/221 t ỷ VND 
(+33%/46% yoy), lần lượt hoàn thành 48%/49% kế hoạch năm.

Chúng tôi dự phóng lượng khách quốc tế/nội địa tại các thị trường AST khai thác tăng 
bình quân 11%/5% mỗi năm đến 2030. Lưu lượng và tỷ trọng khách quốc tế gia tăng 
được kỳ vọng thúc đẩy số giao dịch và doanh thu bình quân trên mỗi khách, đặc biệt 
tại mảng miễn thuế, bán lẻ và phòng chờ.

Chúng tôi dự phóng số điểm kinh doanh AST tăng từ 145 điểm cuối năm 2026 lên 182 
điểm năm 2030. Sau đóng góp t ừ 19 điểm tại T3 Tân Sơn Nh ất và 6 điểm mới tại T2 
Nội Bài từ 2025 - 2026, các dự án Long Thành, Phú Qu ốc và Gia Bình ti ếp tục mở ra 
dư địa mở rộng mạng lưới trong trung và dài hạn cho doanh nghiệp.

Việc mở rộng các hoạt động mang lại lợi nhuận cao, bao gồm hàng miễn thuế, phòng 
chờ thương gia và tối ưu hoạt động tại các điểm kinh doanh đ ược kỳ vọng tác động 
tích c ực lên biên l ợi nhu ận của AST . Chúng tôi dự phóng biên l ợi nhu ận g ộp đ ạt 
64.7%/65.5% trong năm 2026/2027, hỗ trợ LNST công ty mẹ tăng lần lượt 48%/17% 
yoy.

Chúng tôi khuy ến nghị MUA đối với AST , giá mục tiêu 76 ,500 VND/cổ phiếu, tương 
đương tiềm năng tăng giá 20% so với giá đóng cửa ngày 21/09/2026.

Doanh thu 2Q2026 giảm nhẹ do 
không còn ghi nhận doanh thu 1 lần 
từ phí nước thải; trong khi LNST 
công ty mẹ tăng trưởng 1 chữ số

Tiêu thụ nước khu vực dân sinh đư ợc 
thúc đẩy nhờ thời tiết nắng nóng hơn; 
trong khi tri ển v ọng nhóm khách 
hàng công nghi ệp b ị ảnh hư ởng b ởi 
bất lợi thuế quan

Đơn giá thu gom, vận chuyện và xử 
lý rác thải được phê duyệt cho phép 
cải thiện doanh thu và lợi nhuận của 
mảng này

Khuyến nghị MUA với giá mục tiêu 
59,000 VNĐ/cổ phiếu

Doanh thu 2Q2026 đạt 1,222 tỷ (-8% YoY/18.1% QoQ) và LNST công ty mẹ đạt 325 tỷ 
VND (7% YoY/55% QoQ), ghi nhận tăng trưởng của tất cả các mảng. Mảng cung cấp 
nước tăng trưởng ổn định, tiếp tục là trụ cột chính của BWE, đóng góp doanh thu 785 
tỷ VND (+12% YoY/12% QoQ) nhờ nhà máy nước Long An v ận hành từ Q3/2025 giúp 
sản lượng nước tăng 28% YoY.

Cường độ và thời gian kéo dài c ủa các đ ợt nắng nóng trong 2026/2027 là cơ s ở để 
chúng tôi đi ều chỉnh tăng tăng trư ởng tiêu th ụ nước của nhóm khách hàng dân cư 
tại Bình Dương và Bình Phư ớc trong năm 2026 - 2027 lên mức 7.5/6.5% YoY. Trái lại, 
triển vọng tăng trưởng tiêu thụ nước nhóm sản xuất ảm đạm do tình hình thuế quan 
có diễn biến bất lợi trong ngắn hạn cho các ngành xu ất khẩu truyền thống của Việt 
Nam.

UBND TP .HCM công bố đơn giá thu gom, vận chuyển và xử lý rác thải mới, tăng mạnh 
từ 50-150% nhằm phản ánh đầy đủ và chính xác hơn các chi phí phát sinh trong thực 
tế. Chúng tôi cho rằng điều này sẽ tác động tích cực với BWE khi các dự án của doanh 
nghiệp ở mảng này s ẽ vận hành hi ệu quả hơn. Dự báo doanh thu và l ợi nhuận gộp 
của mảng xử lý rác thải lần lượt đạt 1,069/1,234 và 165.7/180.7 tỷ VND lần lượt trong 
2026/2027, tương ứng mức tăng 34%/15% và 27%/9% YoY.

Kết hợp 2 phương pháp định giá FCFF và EV/EBITDA, KBSV khuyến nghị MUA với BWE, 
giá mục tiêu 59,000 VND, tương đương với mức lợi suất kỳ vọng 42.2% so với giá đóng 
cửa ngày 22/09/2026.

F I R S T  T O  M A R K E T

Quan điểm kỹ thuật 
Xu hướng & Hành động

Chỉ số VNINDEX

Source: Trading View, KB Securities Vietnam

Ngắn hạn 
(Dưới 3 tháng)

Trung hạn (Từ 3 đến 9 tháng)

Chỉ báo  Nhận định xu hướng Hành động 
Xanh đậm  Khả năng tăng điểm cao Mua mạnh 
Xanh nhạt  Khả năng tăng điểm vừa phải Mua từng phần 
Vàng  Xu hướng trung tính Mua/Bán trading 
Cam  Khả năng giảm điểm vừa phải Bán từng phần 
Đỏ  Khả năng giảm điểm cao Bán mạnh

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX điều chỉnh trở lại trong tuần qua sau khi gặp thử thách tại vùng cản quanh 1850, 
hình thành cây nến tuần giảm điểm nhưng với phần bóng dưới khá dài cho thấy sự tham 
gia tích cực của lực cầu bắt đáy tại vùng hỗ trợ gần. Trên khung ngày, nhịp giảm mạnh giữa 
tuần đã khiến chỉ số đánh mất đáy ngắn hạn tuần trước và chớm cắt xuống dưới SMA200, 
trong khi phiên hồi phục cuối tuần chưa cho thấy sự cải thiện rõ rệt của lực cầu. Mặc dù 
diễn biến này cho thấy áp lực điều chỉnh vẫn có thể còn tiếp diễn, tuy nhiên khi xu hướng 
tăng ngắn hạn chưa bị phá vỡ, VNINDEX được kỳ vọng sẽ nhận được lực đỡ tại vùng hỗ trợ 
gần quanh 1750, tương ứng vùng hợp lưu quanh SMA50, và có cơ hội hồi phục trở lại. 
Kháng cự xa: 1880 (+-15) 
Kháng cự gần: 1830 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ gần: 1750 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ xa: 1710 (+-15)

### Page 9

9

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

Phương pháp tiếp cận: 
- Danh mục tập hợp các cổ phiếu tiềm năng nhất được KBSV 
lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng. 
- Ngưỡng cắt lỗ cần được cân nhắc cho mỗi mã là -15%. 
- Danh mục giả định luôn duy trì tỷ trọng 100% là cổ phiếu với 
trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

Lưu ý:  
- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn 
giá khuyến nghị mua.

## Document 267: Cliff Notes_ mulling the minutes (2026-10-20)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Cliff Notes_ mulling the minutes (2026-10-20).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-10-20
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#cliffnotes #rba #australia #inflation #us #fomc #ecb #aud #usd
Cliff Notes: mulling the minutes
By Sian Fenner, Ryan Wells & Illiana Jain
11:10 October 09 2026 Share
Key insights from the week that was.
The only data release of note in Australia this week was the October Westpac-MI Consumer Sentiment
Survey, which reported a 4.7% drop in the headline index. At 80.4, sentiment has tumbled back to
deeply pessimistic levels, similar to the post-pandemic cost-of-living shock when readings were among

the weakest in the survey’s more than fifty-year history. Indeed, inflation and interest rate pressures are
once again clearly front of mind for consumers, with both the view on ‘family finances vs a year ago’

### Page 2

and ‘family finance next 12 months’ falling 8.0% and 6.4% in the month, respectively. Moreover, the
RBA’s September rate hike looks to have had a significant impact – sentiment tanked 20% between
those surveyed before the RBA decision and those surveyed afterwards.

Views on the economic outlook were relatively more resilient, but they still weakened, with the one-year
and five-year ahead sub-indexes falling 1.6% and 0.4% respectively. Consumers are becoming more
‘on edge’ about job security, albeit not outright alarmed. The unemployment rate remains relatively low
versus history, although it has drifted up to a cycle high of 4.6% as labour supply continues to outstrip
demand. We expect it to hold there in next week’s September Labour Force Survey – see our preview
for more detail. All told, with higher interest rates, renewed inflation pressures and a softening jobs
market all weighing at once, households are entering the final months of the year on the back foot.

Offshore, the focus was on US data.

Before turning to this week's releases, it is worth noting that September non-farm payrolls came in
softer than expected across most metrics. Employment rose by only 29k (89k expected), and there
were sizeable downward revisions to prior months, with a combined 60k removed from July and August
payrolls, leaving gains of just 31k and 29k respectively. The details pointed to weaker hiring in leisure &
hospitality and education & health services industries, although construction and manufacturing
employment continued to benefit from ongoing data-centre-related investment. The unemployment rate
edged up to 4.2%, with softer employment occurring alongside a rise in labour force participation to
61.8%. Meanwhile wage growth moderated to 0.1%mth (3.0%yr). Taken together, the report suggests
labour demand is cooling, with the three-month average pace of job creation slowing to around 50k.
However, softer payroll gains are not necessarily indicative of labour market deterioration. Lower
immigration and ageing demographics have reduced the pace of employment growth needed to keep
the unemployment rate stable. As such, the report is more consistent with a labour market that is
gradually normalising than one entering a meaningful downturn.

The September FOMC minutes reinforced a distinctly hawkish message, with participants noting they
had "not seen sufficient progress on lowering inflation in recent months" and expressing concern that
inflation risks remained "skewed to the upside". Policymakers highlighted that both core services and
core goods inflation remained elevated, with some attributing persistent goods price strength to the
effects of the AI buildout despite the sector's traditional disinflationary role. There was also concern
that, after more than five years of inflation above target, elevated inflation could begin to influence
inflation expectations and wage- and price-setting behaviour. In contrast, labour market conditions
were generally viewed as stable and "close to maximum employment". While a majority noted the
labour market had strengthened somewhat recently, several participants highlighted unusually low
dynamism, characterised by low hiring, low layoffs, a low job-finding rate and elevated long-term
unemployment. Importantly, participants generally judged that aggregate wage growth was moderate
and "consistent with inflation moving toward 2 percent", with several explicitly noting that "the labor
market was not currently a source of inflationary pressures".

### Page 3

Related articles
A key focus was the interaction between AI investment, productive capacity and inflation. While
participants broadly expected AI-related investment to boost productivity growth and potential output
over time, several warned that "the AI buildout could cause aggregate demand to outpace aggregate
supply over the medium term, putting upward pressure on inflation". Consistent with this view, some
participants had raised their estimate of the neutral policy rate and several judged the current policy
stance to be "not restrictive or only mildly restrictive". Against this backdrop, all participants supported
the September rate increase, and most assessed that another increase in the federal funds rate would
likely be appropriate by year-end, although future decisions would remain data dependent.

The September ISM Services PMI released this week painted a similar picture of an economy that
remains resilient but is gradually losing momentum. The headline index eased to 54.9 from 55.4 in
August, with the decline driven by a sharp fall in business activity and a 9.4pt drop in new export
orders, which slipped into contractionary territory for the first time in eight months. Labour market
conditions improved modestly with the employment index moving to broadly neutral territory 50.1 after
two months below 50, supported by elevated backlogs and still-solid demand. Respondent
commentary illustrated the mixed nature of labour demand, with some firms reporting they were "filling
positions vacated due to promotions or retirements", while others cited "restructuring due to efficiencies
gained using AI tools". Cost pressures also remained elevated, with the prices paid index rising to 74.0,
its highest level since July 2022 and around 18pts above its historical average, while fuel costs, tariffs
and supply chain constraints remained the most frequently cited concerns. Overall, the report suggests
services activity remains consistent with ongoing expansion, albeit at a slower pace, while labour
demand and pricing pressures remain firm.

Overall, the data supports our view that the FOMC will move to a broadly neutral policy stance by the
end of the year with one more rate hike, although the case for a back-to-back move in October has
become less clear cut.

## Document 272: CoreWeave会是2026年的硅谷银行吗？

Source PDF: all report pdf/CoreWeave会是2026年的硅谷银行吗？.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-20
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

巴克莱认为，规模仅 20 亿美元的⽆担保债券遭抛售，并不意味着 AI 融资链条断裂。相较于
五⼤云⼚商今年预计⾼达 7250 亿⾄ 8000 亿美元的 AI 资本开⽀， CoreWeave ⽀出占⽐仅约
4% ⾄ 5% 。真正的⻛险在于算⼒租赁价格下跌，侵蚀企业现⾦流并引发信⽤⻛险扩散。
摘要：
CoreWeave ⽆担保债券收益率飙升⾄ 15% ，重新点燃市场对 AI 基础设施投资周期能否持续的担
忧。但巴克莱认为，投资者可能正在重蹈 2023 年硅⾕银⾏危机时的覆辙，将⼀家公司的局部信
⽤⻛险误读为整个⾏业的系统性危机。
据英国《⾦融时报》，巴克莱全球研究主席 Ajay Rajadhyaksha 指出，CoreWeave ⽆担保债券遭
遇抛售，主要反映其在公司资本结构中处于较低的偿付优先级，⽽⾮ AI 资本⽀出周期出现裂
缝。他认为， Chamath Palihapitiya 和埃隆 · ⻢斯克等⼈过度解读了这批规模仅 20 亿美元的⽆担
保债券所释放的信号。
从市场表现看，此次波动的冲击远不及 2023 年硅⾕银⾏危机期间的全⾯恐慌。纳斯达克 100 指数

上周下跌 5.5% ，本周已有所反弹，其他超⼤规模云计算商的债券收益率也随着美债市场企稳⽽
有所回落。⽬前，市场尚未出现 AI 基础设施融资全⾯收缩的明确信号。
⽆担保债券收益率飙升，不等于融资链条断裂
CoreWeave 的融资结构主要包括有担保贷款、可转换债券和⽆担保债券，三者的偿付优先级与
⻛险差异显著。公司主要通过独⽴特殊⽬的载体（ SPV ）发⾏有担保的延迟提款定期贷款
（ DDTL ），以 GPU 硬件、客⼾合同及数据中⼼租约作为抵押。今年 3 ⽉发⾏的 85 亿美元 DDTL
4.0 ，以 Meta 多年期 “ 照付不议 ” 合同为⽀撑，利差为 SOFR 加 225 个基点，穆迪评级为 A3 。
但 CoreWeave 的融资成本正在上升： 5 ⽉发⾏的 31 亿美元 DDTL 5.0 ，利差扩⼤⾄ SOFR 加 450 个
基点； 8 ⽉发⾏的 26 亿美元 DDTL 5.5 ，利差进⼀步升⾄ SOFR 加 550 个基点。与此同时，公司过
去⼀年累计发⾏约 90 亿美元可转债，票息为 1.75% ⾄ 2.875% 。尽管 9 ⽉⼀笔可转债发⾏规模从

30 亿美元上调⾄ 37 亿美元，但股价下跌正给这⼀融资渠道带来压⼒。
此次遭到抛售的⽆担保债券则处于资本结构末端。 Rajadhyaksha 指出，清算时，这批债券之前
仍有数百亿美元的优先索偿权，因此 15% 的收益率更多反映其⾃⾝的⾼⻛险，⽽⾮整个⾏业的
融资状况。
他以房贷和信⽤卡作⽐：信⽤卡利率⾼，并不意味着整个城市的房贷市场都出了问题。融资成
本上升值得警惕，但不能据此认定 AI 基础设施融资链条已经断裂。
CoreWeave 体量有限，难以撼动万亿美元 AI 投资周期
即便 CoreWeave 陷⼊困境，其对整体 AI 基础设施投资的影响也可能相对有限。 Rajadhyaksha 指
出，公司 2026 年资本⽀出指引为 350 亿⾄ 390 亿美元，⽽微软、 Meta 、 Alphabet 、亚⻢逊和甲⻣
⽂五⼤超⼤规模云计算商今年的 AI 基础设施⽀出预计合计达 7250 亿⾄ 8000 亿美元， 2027 年更有
望突破万亿美元。
按这⼀⼝径， CoreWeave 的⽀出仅占⾏业总量的约 4% ⾄ 5% 。即使公司完全停⽌⽀出，整体 AI

资本⽀出也只会减少约 4% ⾄ 5% ；若⽀出减半，影响则约为 2% 。Rajadhyaksha 认为，即使按
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
97 收藏 分享到 :
写评论
照 2027 年⾏业⽀出超过万亿美元计算， CoreWeave ⽀出减半所造成的缺⼝也约为 200 亿美元，
不⾜以终结整个投资周期。
市场担忧的另⼀层⻛险是信⽤传染： CoreWeave 若出现问题，是否意味着⾕歌等超⼤规模云计
算商也将⾯临融资困境？ Rajadhyaksha 认为，这⼀推论跨越了太多环节。微软、 Meta 、
Alphabet 和亚⻢逊拥有更强的⾃由现⾦流⽀撑，对⽆担保债券市场的依赖程度也较低。
⼀家⾼度依赖外部融资的算⼒供应商出现信⽤⻛险，并不意味着整个 AI 投资链条都将陷⼊融资困
境。
真正的⻛险信号：算⼒价格是否崩塌
Rajadhyaksha 指出，真正值得警惕的不是 CoreWeave 的债券收益率，⽽是算⼒价格是否⼤幅下
跌。 CoreWeave 等 AI 基础设施公司的商业模式，本质上是购⼊ GPU 、出租算⼒，以租⾦收⼊覆

盖融资成本、设备折旧和运营⽀出。⼀旦算⼒租赁价格下跌，收⼊不⾜以覆盖成本，企业的盈
利能⼒与偿债能⼒就可能同步恶化， AI 投资周期才会⾯临更实质性的挑战。
⽬前，供应链数据尚未明确指向这⼀情景。台积电的业绩反映芯⽚需求仍在增⻓， ASML 的订单
积压显⽰相关设备需求依然强劲，超⼤规模云计算商的资本⽀出计划也尚未显露明显退缩。
CoreWeave ⾃⾝披露的合同积压订单⾼达 1040 亿美元，其中包含 “ 照付不议 ” 性质的合同，为未
来收⼊提供了⼀定⽀撑，但并不能完全消除融资与履约⻛险。
Rajadhyaksha 认为，从 CoreWeave 债券收益率升⾄ 15% ，直接推导出 AI 基础设施融资已经断
裂、资本⽀出周期即将结束，缺乏充分依据。本周市场的反弹也暂未印证最悲观的判断。不
过， AI 交易已成为左右市场⾛势的重要⼒量，投资者对任何可能动摇这⼀叙事的信号保持⾼度
敏感，并不意外。
CoreWeave 会不会成为 2026 年的硅⾕银⾏，关键不在于⼀批低优先级债券的收益率有多⾼，⽽

在于融资压⼒是否会进⼀步传导⾄算⼒价格、企业现⾦流和整个⾏业的资本开⽀。  巴克莱认
为，市场可能⾼估了此次债券抛售所代表的⾏业⻛险，但算⼒需求与租赁价格能否持续，仍是
判断 AI 投资周期⾛向的关键。
